**2023, October 24** — 2:41 a.m. At 0241Z on 2023-10-24, a military observer reported seeing one solid UAP in the Arabian Gulf near 39RYJ74 / 39RXK68 at estimated radius 5, while flying at FL243 on a 280-degree trajectory. The object was assessed as benign, under no intelligent control, and had a thermal signature that appeared cold; no maneuverability, engagement, sensory effects, or recovered material were reported. ([DOW-UAP-D023, pp. 7–8](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/dow-uap-d23-mission-report-united-arab-emirates-october-2023.pdf>))

**2023, October 24** — 3:22 a.m. At 0322Z on 2023-10-24, a military observer reported seeing one solid UAP near 39RXL60 in the Arabian Gulf/USCENTCOM area while flying at FL243 on a 280-degree trajectory at 162 KTAS. The report characterizes the object as benign, with no observed maneuverability, no intelligent control, no reported sound, lights, electromagnetic effects, or physical effects, and lists the UAP as unknown in propulsion, signature, and detailed description. ([DOW-UAP-D023, pp. 8–9](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/dow-uap-d23-mission-report-united-arab-emirates-october-2023.pdf>))

**2023, October 27** — 12:35 a.m. At 0035Z on 2023-10-27, while an Air Force ISR aircraft en route to its target over the Aegean Sea was operating near 35SKD591.4531 / 35S KD 95 53, the crew observed one possible UAP just above the ocean surface. The object was described as seemingly circular, too small to resolve details, moving at an estimated 80 mph and making multiple sharp 90-degree turns before being lost from the feed at 0038Z. The observer assessed it as benign and noted no response to observer actions. ([DOW-UAP-D033, pp. 1–7](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/dow-uap-d33-mission-report-greece-october-2023.pdf>))

**2023, October 29** — 8:11 a.m. At 0811Z on 29 Oct 2023, an Air Force ISR aircraft (callsign 1.4a) returning to base over the Aegean Sea observed one possible UAP just above the ocean surface, moving straight toward land; the object was described as seemingly circular and too small for detail, was lost from the feed moments later, and no EEI-related activity, sounds, lights, or responses were reported. ([DOW-UAP-D035, pp. 1–7](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/dow-uap-d35-mission-report-greece-october-2023.pdf>))

**2023, October** — 6:58 p.m. Witness 5, a U.S. federal law enforcement special agent, reported multiple nighttime sightings in the western United States in October 2023 while hiking and driving near a wildlife area and later near a lake/base road. They observed a hovering orange light over a ridge, then a red orb that appeared to release two smaller red orbs and a white flash, and later a red-and-white light pair that hovered low over the road, drifted into the sand and bushes, and collapsed into a thin horizontal line before vanishing. The team used NVGs and thermal binoculars, set up a camera, communicated by radio with other teams, and returned the next morning to search the area, noting unusual ground scrapes near a plant. ([DOW-UAP-D083, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/061226/release_03/documents/DoW-UAP-D083_Narrative-5_Western-US-Event.pdf>))

**2023, October 2–3** — 7:00 p.m. Witness 4, a U.S. federal law enforcement special agent, reported multiple UAP sightings over several days in western U.S. desert/mountain terrain beginning in October 2023. The witness described bright orange/yellow-white lights, orbs, lines of lights, clustered patterns, and at least one darker object with a faint red light seen hovering between ridges; some lights appeared to vanish, reappear, form geometric shapes, or move in ways the witness considered inconsistent with ordinary aircraft or vehicles. The observations were made both with naked eye and NVGs, often near ridgelines and hillsides, sometimes at close apparent range, and included one later instance of a bright light moving north-south and disappearing behind a hillside. ([DOW-UAP-D082, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/061226/release_03/documents/DoW-UAP-D082_Narrative-4_Western-US-Event.pdf>))

**2023, October** — 7:00 p.m. In October 2023, a federal law enforcement witness and partner conducting drone surveillance in the western United States reported three connected but distinct incidents involving bright orb-like lights, red lights, and vehicle-like objects that appeared to mimic headlights, taillights, a box truck, and a semi-truck. In Incident 1, multiple bright orb-like lights repeatedly appeared over the northern/eastern horizon and expelled three or four red lights that moved into a coordinated horizontal formation before the main light dimmed; the red lights later hovered over the southern horizon for hours. In Incident 2, red lights hovered over the southern horizon while nearby vehicle-like lights morphed into a bright orb and then a box-truck-shaped cluster of lights, accelerating instantly and gliding smoothly across rugged terrain. In Incident 3, a single orb-like light moved along a rural highway, dimmed into a red taillight, then reappeared as an orange orb/disc before a semi-truck-like object passed at high speed; the witness reported no sounds, radio issues, or physical effects in any incident. ([DOW-UAP-D080, pp. 1–8](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/061226/release_03/documents/DoW-UAP-D080_Narrative-2_Western-US-Event.pdf>))

**2023, October** — Illustration summarizing four reported UAP incidents in the western United States during October 2023, reportedly observed by U.S. federal law enforcement special agents. The incidents included an orange 'mother orb' launching smaller red orbs, a large fiery orb projected against a ridgeline at about 1,000 yards, a thin or dark kite-shaped object with one red and one white light at close range, and a translucent kite-shaped object at close range. ([DOW-UAP-D078, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/061226/release_03/documents/DOW-UAP-D078_Notional-Map_Western-United-States-Event.pdf>))

**2023, October** — Over two days in October 2023 near a sensitive national security site in the western United States, six federal law enforcement special agents reported seeing repeated orange and red luminous orbs at dusk. The agents described an orange 'mother orb' that appeared for one to two seconds, allegedly released clusters of two to four smaller red orbs, then vanished. The red orbs were described as silent, anomalous in motion, sometimes moving horizontally or changing altitude, and in at least one account one orb remained stationary above a ridgeline for several hours. AARO reviewed the case in 2026 and judged part of the report plausibly attributable to military flares, with the remainder unresolved and possibly representing unrecognized technology. ([DOW-UAP-D077, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/061226/release_03/documents/DOW-UAP-D077_Unresolved-Case-Analysis-Update_Western-United-States-Event.pdf>))

**2023, November 9** — 9:53 p.m. On 2023-11-09 at about 21:53Z over Syria, a military aircrew member (call sign 1.4a) reported a single probable hostile-capability UAP shaped like a bouncy ball approaching from the south at near co-altitude while RTB. The object was assessed as benign, dropped altitude, and safely passed the aircraft while maintaining an estimated ~424 knots for about 7 minutes before leaving range; no emissions or effects on the aircrew were reported. ([DOW-UAP-D074, p. 10](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/dow-uap-d74-mission-report-syria-november-2023.pdf>))

**2023** — In the western United States on two separate dusk observations in 2023, six federal law enforcement special agents working in three independent two-person teams reported seeing orange orbs in the sky briefly appear, launch groups of smaller red orbs, and then vanish. The launches reportedly occurred at least five times, with the orange orb visible for only one or two seconds each time; the red orbs generally moved horizontally away from the larger orb, though some witnesses described occasional angled ascent or a swooping descent. The report is compiled by the Department of War from multiple independent witness statements and also notes other unrelated categories of anomalous observations. ([Western US Event, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/western_us_event_slides_5.08.2026.pdf>))

**2023** — 4:00 a.m. In the western U.S. during pre-dawn hours in 2023, two federal law enforcement special agents observed what first appeared to be a car with red and white lights traveling on a road in a restricted desert zone. As they closed to within a few hundred feet, the object moved laterally off-road over the desert without changing orientation, remained at roughly the same height, and seemed to move with no resistance at an estimated 15-20 mph. It stopped about 100 meters from the road, extinguished its lights, and was briefly observed in night vision goggles as a thin horizontal line or dark, kite-like/triangular shape about 4 feet wide before moving again and rising in height while still appearing as a flat line, then disappearing after a few seconds. ([Western US Event, p. 3](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/western_us_event_slides_5.08.2026.pdf>))

**2023** — 3:00 a.m. About 30 minutes after the earlier "Dark Kite" event, federal law enforcement special agents USPER5 and USPER6, with USPER7 present, returned to the same general area in the western U.S. during pre-dawn hours responding to another report of an unauthorized object. Near the prior sighting location, USPER5 (using NVGs) and USPER6 (bare eye) observed a kite-shaped object with a similar lighting pattern, about 6 meters above the ground, canted from lower right to upper left and drifting slowly with the wind. USPER5 thought the object appeared somewhat transparent because faint stars could be seen through it in night vision. After the object was lost, USPER5 used a spotlight and briefly perceived the beam as being blocked at about 50 yards before it again projected normally, but the team could not reacquire the object. ([Western US Event, p. 4](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/western_us_event_slides_5.08.2026.pdf>))

**2023** — Two federal law enforcement special agents in the western United States reported seeing a glowing orange orb at dusk, hovering near a rock pinnacle at an estimated 500-600 meters (later assessed by AARO to be about 1050 meters away). The object was described as roughly the size of a small helicopter cockpit, with no clear structure visible aside from a possible small spindle underneath, and it emitted no noticeable sound. The sighting lasted about a minute. ([Western US Event, p. 2](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/western_us_event_slides_5.08.2026.pdf>))

**2024, January 25** — 5:09 a.m. A 33 SOS ISR mission over the Mediterranean Sea produced a UAP sighting at 0509Z on 2024-01-25 while the aircraft was in transit. The observer reported one solid, diamond-shaped object with a straight, non-maneuvering tail/probe, seen only on the SWIR camera and appearing white; it was estimated at about 434 knots, maintained a steady flight path with altitude changes, and the event lasted about 2 minutes without any other incident. ([DOW-UAP-D025, pp. 1–7](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/dow-uap-d25-mission-report-greece-january-2024.pdf>))

**2024, February 1** — 11:25 a.m. In June 2024 a former U.S. Army intelligence officer was interviewed at 26 Federal Plaza in New York for a forensic sketch reconstruction of a UAP he had observed over Cheyenne Mountain in February 2024 at about 11:25 am. He and four other members of his unit saw a potato-shaped object in a clear blue-sky day over the mountains: creamy/whitish, opalescent, slightly translucent, and shimmering, with irregular articulating panel-like scales that shifted in slow waves while the craft itself remained stationary. After about two minutes it vanished/cloaked abruptly with no shadow. ([FBI-UAP-D002, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/061226/release_03/documents/FBI-UAP-D002_FD-1057_Unresolved-UAP-Report_ColoradoSprings_2022.pdf>))

**2024, June 7** — 4:57 a.m. During a June 2024 ISR mission out of OMAM in the Gulf of Oman / UAE area, a PRUVE aircraft on RTB visually detected one UAP at 0457Z. The object was described as a glowing hot spherical unidentified object with a vertical unwavering cylindrical pole or bar attached to the bottom, possibly reflecting in the water. The observer assessed it as benign; it moved straight over the water at about 140 knots, with no change in response to observer actions and no reported radar, communication, or physical effects. ([DOW-UAP-D027, pp. 1–8](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/dow-uap-d27-mission-report-united-arab-emirates-october-2023.pdf>))

**2024, July 14** — 2:05 p.m. On 2024-07-14, a U.S. Air Force ISR aircraft from 124 ATKS operating over the Gulf of Aden observed one UAP during a planned mission after coordinating with NAVCENT. The object was first seen at 140517Z while the aircraft was on station at 140300Z and followed until it became too distant to track. The observer described a straight flight path at the same altitude, faster-than-aircraft-speed movement, and assessed the object as benign. ([DOW-UAP-D075, pp. 1–8](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/dow-uap-d75-mission-report-gulf-of-aden-july-2024.pdf>))

**2024, September 20** — 8:27 p.m. During an armed overwatch mission from OKAS over Ayn al Asad Airbase's ROZ Raindrop, a crew observed an unidentified aerial phenomenon while employing an AGM-176 after a weapons calibration. The object appeared in MX-20/MX-25 infrared sensors as a high-speed IR lens flare or significant heat source, moving through the sensor field of view between munition release and impact; the crew did not reobserve it and assessed the event as benign. ([DOW-UAP-D028, pp. 1–6](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/dow-uap-d28-mission-report-east-china-sea-2024.pdf>))

**2024, October 20** — 3:59 p.m. On 2024-10-20 at 15:59Z over Syria, a military FMV feed showed multiple flashes/glare from an unknown origin crossing the camera image at different angles; aircrew described the phenomenon as a mishapen, uneven ball of white light and assessed it as benign with no mission impact and no change in response. ([DOW-UAP-D032, pp. 7–9](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/dow-uap-d32-mission-report,-syria-october-2024.pdf>))

**2024, October 31** — On 31 Oct 2024, a U.S. aircraft reportedly observed a possible UAP that was described as oval/orb-shaped and likely moving slowly. The aircraft reportedly maintained visual contact for over 2 hours. The report appears in heavily redacted email correspondence requesting approval to include the year of the incident date in an unclassified tear line. ([DOW-UAP-D052, pp. 1–2](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/dow-uap-d52-email-correspondance-na-august-2024.pdf>))

**2024, October** — FBI electronic communication from October 2024 documents repeated observations near the witness's residence of unknown UxS/UAP described as plasma orbs or anomalous lights. The witness reported multiple video clips of renewed activity, with observations dating back to November 2021, including hovering and slow movement near the trees, ground level, horizon, and water, sometimes as close as 200 yards from the home. The witness also reported trail cameras, other sensors, gamma radiation spikes correlated with sightings, and unusual effects on electronics and GPS, while seeking privacy and not wanting the matter made public. ([FBI-UAP-D004, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/061226/release_03/documents/FBI-UAP-D004_FD-1057-02_Northeastern-US_2024.pdf>))

**2024, November 2** — 4:45 p.m. FBI FD-1057 electronic communication documents a UAP observation follow-up visit on 2024-11-02 at a home overlooking a pond and wooded area. Special Agents and the resident observed several light phenomena over the water and tree line between about 4:45 pm and 6:10 pm, including a white pulsing light moving erratically and disappearing, stationary pulsing white/blue lights at the tree line, and later a bright white light above a red light that hovered near tree-top height and moved quickly leftward before vanishing and reappearing. Five blurry photos were taken as attempted documentation. ([FBI-UAP-D007, pp. 1–4](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/061226/release_03/documents/FBI-UAP-D007_FD-1057-06_Northeastern-US_2024.pdf>))

**2024, November** — FBI Special Agents conducted a site survey in November 2024 at a private home/property where the resident reported seeing unidentified aerial phenomenon over the last three years. The resident gave agents a tour of the property, showed a wooded area about 200 yards from the home with a trail camera facing west, and pointed out areas where UAP activity had been seen. The report describes photos from treeline, trail camera, and back deck as enclosures, but does not provide a direct description of the objects themselves. ([FBI-UAP-D006, pp. 1–3](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/061226/release_03/documents/FBI-UAP-D006_FD-1057-05_Northeastern-US_2024.pdf>))

**2024, December** — 10:30 a.m. FBI Special Agents conducted a site survey in 12/2024 of areas where UAP activity had reportedly been observed in 11/2024; they walked a marked trail and descended to the water's edge to photograph and inspect the locations, finding no unusual ground or treetop activity and concluding the tree cover would make nighttime drone or craft flight unrealistic. ([FBI-UAP-D008, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/061226/release_03/documents/FBI-UAP-D008_FD-1057-07_Northeastern-US_2024.pdf>))

**2025, April 10** — 11:53 p.m. On 10 Apr 2025 at 2353Z, a U.S. aircraft reportedly observed one possible UAP for 12 seconds at unknown altitude and speed; no interference was noted. The document is heavily redacted email correspondence about a mission report and asks for clarification on its contents. ([DOW-UAP-D050, p. 2](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/dow-uap-d50-email-correspondence-indopacom-april-2025.pdf>))

**2025, April 11** — 12:07 a.m. On 11 Apr 2025 at 0007Z, a US aircraft reportedly observed one possible UAP for 23 seconds at unknown altitude and speed. No interference was noted. The available text is a heavily redacted email correspondence referencing a mission report and requesting clarification on its contents. ([DOW-UAP-D050, p. 2](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/dow-uap-d50-email-correspondence-indopacom-april-2025.pdf>))

**2025, May 7** _(cluster ×2)_ — 9:05 p.m. On 7 May 2025 at 2105, four reliable observers at Camp Hood, Texas reported a green-white diamond-shaped object at an apparent altitude of 1300 feet. It was observed for about 40 minutes, appeared about 3 mils wide, traveled horizontally about 15 mils in that interval, made no sound and no trail, then dimmed and went out. ([65_HS1-834228961_62-HQ-83894_Section_006, p. 45](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/65_hs1-834228961_62-hq-83894_section_6.pdf>); [DOW-UAP-D017, p. 36](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/052226/release_02/documents/DOW-UAP-D017_General_Correspondence_Of_Sandia.pdf>))

**2025, December 31** _(cluster ×6)_ — 6:11 p.m. A redacted FBI-submitted still sensor image from a U.S. military system shows a small dark circular object in the upper-right quadrant against a grainy monochrome background with an indistinct mountain range and targeting reticle; the operator could not positively identify the object, and no mission report accompanied the image. ([FBI Photo B012, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b12.pdf>); [FBI Photo B002, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b2.pdf>); [FBI Photo B010, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b10.pdf>); [FBI Photo B011, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b11.pdf>); [FBI Photo B003, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b3.pdf>); [FBI Photo B008, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b8.pdf>))

**2025, December 31** _(cluster ×5)_ — 6:19 p.m. An FBI-submitted redacted sensor still image from a U.S. military system shows a grainy monochrome target display with a crosshair reticle centered on a small dark object or objects near the reticle; the operator could not positively identify the UAP, and the image date/time is noted as incorrect because the system clock was not set. ([FBI Photo B018, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b18.pdf>); [FBI Photo B013, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b13.pdf>); [FBI Photo B020, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b20.pdf>); [FBI Photo B021, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b21.pdf>); [FBI Photo B023, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b23.pdf>))

**2025** — 6:10 p.m. A redacted FBI-submitted sensor still image from a U.S. military system shows a grainy monochrome scene with a targeting reticle and two dark objects, one larger object in the upper right quadrant resembling a helicopter and a second smaller circular object below the reticle; the operator reportedly could not positively identify the UAP. The image date is noted as incorrect due to system time not being set, and the report is associated with late 2025 in the Western United States. ([FBI Photo B007, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b7.pdf>))

**2025** — 6:20 p.m. A redacted FBI-submitted sensor still image from a U.S. military system shows a grainy monochrome scene with a central crosshair and two dark irregular objects just right of center in the upper-right quadrant. The operator could not positively identify the object(s), and no mission report accompanied the image. The image dateline is noted as incorrect because the system date/time was not set. ([FBI Photo B016, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b16.pdf>))

**2025** _(cluster ×2)_ — In late 2025, a senior U.S. intelligence officer, two pilots, and a colleague flew a helicopter from a Joint Operations Center over a remote military test range to investigate loud thuds and prior nights of orb reports. During daylight they repeatedly inspected debris later identified as rocket/projectile wreckage, found a large cave entrance, and later—after sunset—continued searching with FLIR/NVG support. Radar then led them to a series of close UAP encounters: a low, hot object reported by ground teams rose from the ground, approached within about ten feet of the helicopter, split into two, and sped away. Minutes later, they observed swarms of orange orbs at a distance and then several orb formations near the helicopter and above fighter jets, including a stationary cluster in a T-formation and repeated flare-up/dimming events. ([USPER Statement about UAP Sighting, pp. 1–3](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/usper-statement-redacted.pdf>); [ODNI-UAP-D001, pp. 1–2](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/052226/release_02/documents/ODNI-UAP-D001_USPER_Narrative_Senior_USIC.pdf>))

**2025** — A redacted monochrome sensor still from a U.S. military system shows a single dark irregular object near the center of a targeting reticle; the operator could not positively identify it, no mission report was provided, and the image date is known to be incorrect because system time/date were not set. ([FBI Photo B024, p. 1](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/fbi-photo-b24.pdf>))

**2026, February** — 9:15 p.m. Two witnesses reported that on a February 2026 night around 9:15 PM EST, while returning home and pulling into a downhill driveway at their residence, they saw an intense bright light hovering below the tree line in the backyard. The light was described as a brilliant red sphere about one meter across with a white plasma-like center, which moved upward and left, then was joined by a second identical orb. The two silent orbs moved west above the trees in smooth tandem motion, appearing to merge or overlap before going out of sight. A few weeks later, the witness reported seeing several smaller white orbs travel from west to east at much higher altitude over the house. ([FBI-UAP-D009, pp. 1–2](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/061226/release_03/documents/FBI-UAP-D009_FD-302-67_Northeastern-Orb-Sighting_2026.pdf>))

**2030, August 10** — On 10 Aug 2030, a single witness at Killeen Base, Texas reported a white object moving from north to south about 30 degrees above the horizon. The object was seen for about five seconds, appeared similar to a comet, produced no sound, and then disappeared. ([65_HS1-834228961_62-HQ-83894_Section_006, p. 50](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/65_hs1-834228961_62-hq-83894_section_6.pdf>))

**2040, August 10** — 8:40 p.m. On 10 Aug 2040 at Camp Hood, Texas, a single observer reported an orange object with a white tail moving from north to south at about 30 degrees above the horizon in a horizontal path with a slight arc; it was seen for only 3 to 5 seconds and its light gradually diminished, resembling a falling star or rocket/tracer round. ([65_HS1-834228961_62-HQ-83894_Section_006, p. 51](<https://www.war.gov/medialink/ufo/release_1/65_hs1-834228961_62-hq-83894_section_6.pdf>))
